Second transcription's note: Every STOP in Steps 1-5 and Worksheets A/B gives zero. A filer whose EIC was earlier reduced or disallowed (Form 8862) is refused. The 'IRS figures the credit' option is not modeled.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 40, Step 1, question 1: "If, in 2025: 3 or more children who have valid SSNs lived with you, is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than $61,555 ($68,675 if married filing jointly)? ... No. STOP You can't take the credit." i1040gi p. 40, Step 2, question 2: "Is your investment income more than $11,950? Yes. Continue". Triage executable-triage-v2 (triage/eic.json; 1,099 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 132 partial records; 1,731 comparisons): 1,628 of 1,731 agree; no value difference; 41 candidate-unknown (8 on completed returns), 51 candidate-refuses, 8 project-unknown, 3 project-refuses. The candidate-unknown come from its helper eic.h_pre, which needs the child count (for its income limit) and investment income (f1040.line2a) before the Step 1, question 1 STOP that line 11b alone decides when it is at or above the largest limit; the project tests that limit first (eic.below_top) and the interview does not ask those answers there. A candidate that needs answers the source does not need to determine the result is a model error (README). Witnesses: classm.f4952.1 (head of household, line 11b $388,706.00) project 0, candidate unknown; audit.household.hoh_married_no_community_property (completed; line 11b $120,000.00) candidate unknown (dependent eic_ssn and us_half, f1040.tax_exempt_interest, eic.qc_of_another not supplied). The 51 candidate-refuses are scope: its declared gates on eic.h_pre (sa.excluded_income, scope.other_line16) and the dependent other_claimant and married gates it reaches through the child count fire only on returns the project refuses (classm.retirement.3, classm.s179.3, classm.tcw.10). The 3 project-refuses are cited gates (person[taxpayer].f8889[hsa].unsupported, retirement[p].special, and sa.dependent, i1040gi p. 42, Step 4, question 6, on stage9.eic.refuse.dependent_of_another), scope. The 8 project-unknown (stage14.ho.gross_limit, audit.sa.negative_agi_medical) are incomplete records that withhold f1040.line2a, the Step 2 investment-income answer the interview asks of every filer; the candidate reaches 0 there only because both of its worksheet paths are false (representation). Project stands.

Second transcription's note: Covers each STOP that tells you to check line 27c: Step 1 question 2; Step 3 question 6; Step 4 questions 3 and 5; Worksheet A line 2 zero; Worksheet B line 4b zero or less, line 5 'No', and line 7 zero. The nonresident-alien case is refused. The filer's choice not to claim the credit has no supplied input, so it is not modeled.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 43, Line 27c: "Check the box on line 27c if you do not want to claim the earned income credit or if you have been instructed to check the box in the instructions for line 27a." i1040gi p. 40, Step 1, question 1: "If, in 2025: 3 or more children who have valid SSNs lived with you, is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than $61,555 ($68,675 if married filing jointly)? ... No. STOP You can't take the credit." Triage executable-triage-v2 (triage/eic.json; 1,099 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 132 partial records; 1,731 comparisons): 1,631 of 1,731 agree; no value difference; 48 candidate-unknown (13 on completed returns), 51 candidate-refuses, 1 project-refuses. The candidate's 27c conditions read eic.h_pre and eic.h_agi_ok, so it inherits the same unknowns. The candidate-unknown come from its helper eic.h_pre, which needs the child count (for its income limit) and investment income (f1040.line2a) before the Step 1, question 1 STOP that line 11b alone decides when it is at or above the largest limit; the project tests that limit first (eic.below_top) and the interview does not ask those answers there. A candidate that needs answers the source does not need to determine the result is a model error (README). Witnesses: classm.f4952.1 (head of household, line 11b $388,706.00) project 0, candidate unknown; audit.household.hoh_married_no_community_property (completed; line 11b $120,000.00) candidate unknown (dependent eic_ssn and us_half, f1040.tax_exempt_interest, eic.qc_of_another not supplied). The 51 candidate-refuses are scope: its declared gates on eic.h_pre (sa.excluded_income, scope.other_line16) and the dependent other_claimant and married gates it reaches through the child count fire only on returns the project refuses (classm.retirement.3, classm.s179.3, classm.tcw.10). The project refusal (stage9.eic.refuse.dependent_of_another, candidate no) is the cited sa.dependent gate (i1040gi p. 42, Step 4, question 6; scope). Project stands.

Second transcription's note: Step 3 question 6 'No' says to check the box. The Special rule for separated spouses applies only if you are married and not filing a joint return, so the box is limited to married MFS/HOH filers.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 41, Step 3, question 6: "No. Check the box in the Dependents section that discusses the special rule for separated spouses"; i1040gi p. 44, Special rule for separated spouses: "If you are married, but not filing a joint return, had a qualifying child who lived with you ... If you meet these requirements, make sure you check the box in the Dependents section that discusses the special rule for separated spouses". Triage executable-triage-v2 (triage/eic.json; 1,099 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 132 partial records; 1,731 comparisons): 1,675 of 1,731 agree; no value difference; 3 candidate-unknown (1 on a completed return), 51 candidate-refuses, 2 project-refuses. The candidate now limits the box to married filers filing separately or as head of household (eic.h_married_ns), as the project does, but conditions it on eic.h_pre. The candidate-unknown come from its helper eic.h_pre, which needs the child count (for its income limit) and investment income (f1040.line2a) before the Step 1, question 1 STOP that line 11b alone decides when it is at or above the largest limit; the project tests that limit first (eic.below_top) and the interview does not ask those answers there. A candidate that needs answers the source does not need to determine the result is a model error (README). Witnesses: classm.f4952.1 (head of household, line 11b $388,706.00) project 0, candidate unknown; audit.household.hoh_married_no_community_property (completed; line 11b $120,000.00) candidate unknown (dependent eic_ssn and us_half, f1040.tax_exempt_interest, eic.qc_of_another not supplied). The 51 candidate-refuses are scope: its declared gates on eic.h_pre (sa.excluded_income, scope.other_line16) and the dependent other_claimant and married gates it reaches through the child count fire only on returns the project refuses (classm.retirement.3, classm.s179.3, classm.tcw.10). The 2 project-refuses are cited gates (HSA unsupported, retirement special) on refused returns. Project stands.

Second transcription's note: Clergy income is outside the tool's scope (refused), so the line 27b box is never checked.

Ruling: representation; i1040gi p. 43, Line 27b: "Check the box on line 27b if you are (1) a minister, member of a religious order who has not taken a vow of poverty, or a Christian Science practitioner; and (2) filing Schedule SE and the amount on line 2 of that schedule includes an amount that was also reported on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 1z." Clergy income is refused by scope.schedule1 ("including statutory-employee wages or clergy income"); the candidate's constant no with that refusal equals the project. No. STOP You can't take the credit." i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A: "Use this worksheet only if you answered “No” to Step 5, question 2. Otherwise, use Worksheet B." i1040gi p. 43, Step 6, question 1: "No. Go to Worksheet A." The candidate shows Worksheet A line 1 (Step 5 earned income) on every return, including returns that STOP before Step 6 and Worksheet B filers; the project hides it unless Steps 1 to 5 pass (eic.eligible). Witness: classm.f2441.2.deepseek (completed; married filing jointly, two children, line 11b $85,000.00, not less than $64,430) hidden, candidate $85,000.00. The value differences on returns the project completes or leaves incomplete are all Worksheet B filers (eic.self_employed true): the project's printed line 1 also carries Worksheet B line 6 (cite: "Worksheet A lines 2 to 6 are Worksheet B lines 7 to 11"), which the candidate's Worksheet A line cannot express; interface gap, as in round 2. The value differences on refused returns are Worksheet B values or lines whose shown_when itself refuses or is unknown (classm.sc.1: eic.eligible refuses on person[spouse].f8889[hsa].excess), which triage counts as shown; no amount is filed (scope). Second transcription's note: Amounts below $1 or at or above the limit are outside the table and give no credit. The table_lookup status column is assumed to send MFS to the single/HOH/QSS column (★).

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 40, Step 1, question 1: "If, in 2025: 3 or more children who have valid SSNs lived with you, is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than $61,555 ($68,675 if married filing jointly)? ... No. STOP You can't take the credit." i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A: "Use this worksheet only if you answered “No” to Step 5, question 2. Otherwise, use Worksheet B." i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A, line 2: "If line 2 is zero, STOP You can't take the credit." The candidate looks up line 2 on every return; the project hides it unless Steps 1 to 5 pass. Triage executable-triage-v2 (triage/eic.json; 1,099 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 132 partial records; 1,731 comparisons): 1,375 of 1,731 agree; 64 candidate-shows-skipped (26 on completed returns), 44 value, 49 candidate-unknown, 26 candidate-refuses, 101 project-unknown, 72 project-refuses. Witness: classm.sd.1 (completed; head of household, line 11b $168,227.38, Step 1 question 1 STOP) hidden, candidate $2,798.00. The candidate-unknown are its eic.h_limit and child count needed above every Step 1 limit (classm.f4952.1), a model error as on eic.credit. The value differences on returns the project completes or leaves incomplete are all Worksheet B filers (eic.self_employed true): the project's printed line 2 also carries Worksheet B line 7 (cite: "Worksheet A lines 2 to 6 are Worksheet B lines 7 to 11"), which the candidate's Worksheet A line cannot express; interface gap, as in round 2. The value differences on refused returns are Worksheet B values or lines whose shown_when itself refuses or is unknown (classm.sc.1: eic.eligible refuses on person[spouse].f8889[hsa].excess), which triage counts as shown; no amount is filed (scope). Witnesses: classm.s8812.3 (completed) project $300.00 (Worksheet B line 7), candidate $0.00; classm.f8880.1 project $0.00, candidate $2,798.00. The project-unknown and project-refuses are on returns the project refuses (scope). The project-refuses are cited gates on the Worksheet B side of the merged line (s179.multi, person[taxpayer].se_screened, Schedule C vehicle and home-office gates) and the candidate-refuses are s8812.combat_pay and the dependent other_claimant and married gates, all on returns the project refuses (scope).

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 40, Step 1, question 1: "If, in 2025: 3 or more children who have valid SSNs lived with you, is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than $61,555 ($68,675 if married filing jointly)? ... No. STOP You can't take the credit." i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A: "Use this worksheet only if you answered “No” to Step 5, question 2. Otherwise, use Worksheet B." i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A, line 6: "This is your earned income credit." The candidate figures line 6 on every return; the project shows it only when Steps 1 to 5 pass. Triage executable-triage-v2 (triage/eic.json; 1,099 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 132 partial records; 1,731 comparisons): 1,596 of 1,731 agree; 24 candidate-shows-skipped (11 on completed returns), 32 value, 49 candidate-unknown, 30 candidate-refuses. Witness: stage13.eic_spouse_no_ssn (completed; Step 1, question 2 "No. STOP", no valid SSN for the spouse) hidden, candidate $649.00. The candidate-unknown are its child count above every Step 1 limit (classm.f4952.1), as on eic.credit. The value differences on returns the project completes or leaves incomplete are all Worksheet B filers (eic.self_employed true): the project's printed line 6 also carries Worksheet B line 11 (cite: "Worksheet A lines 2 to 6 are Worksheet B lines 7 to 11"), which the candidate's Worksheet A line cannot express; interface gap, as in round 2. The value differences on refused returns are Worksheet B values or lines whose shown_when itself refuses or is unknown (classm.sc.1: eic.eligible refuses on person[spouse].f8889[hsa].excess), which triage counts as shown; no amount is filed (scope). Witnesses: classm.s8812.3 project $300.00, candidate $0.00; classm.sli.4 project $0.00 (Worksheet B line 4b zero or less), candidate $503.00. The candidate-refuses are on refused returns (scope).

## Refusal gates listed by the second transcription

Reported, not judged: the project often refuses before a worksheet is used, on the line that chooses it, which a per-line comparison cannot see.

| line | gates listed | not read by the project's line |
| --- | --- | --- |
| eic.credit | household.credit_disallowed, s8812.combat_pay, sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16, scope.schedule1 | - |
| eic.check_27c | s8812.combat_pay, sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16, scope.schedule1 | - |
| eic.separated_box | sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16, scope.schedule1 | - |
| eic.clergy | scope.schedule1 | - |
| (Step 5, 1) | scope.schedule1 | - |
| (Step 5, 2) | scope.other_income | - |
| (Step 5, 3) | scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |
| (Step 5, 4) | s8812.combat_pay | - |
| (Step 5, 5) | s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |
| 4b | s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |
| 6 (eic.wsb_6) | s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |
| 1 | s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |
| 2 | s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |
| 4 | s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |
| 6 (eic.line6) | s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: eic.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero amount (for blank, -0-, or STOP results)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}

  - id: eic.step5_line1
    printed: "(Step 5, 1)"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 1z"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line1z}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1]
    note: "The Clergy and Church employees adjustments to this line are open. Clergy and church employee income are outside the tool's scope (scope.schedule1), so the line is Form 1040 line 1z unchanged."

  - id: eic.step5_line2
    printed: "(Step 5, 2)"
    label: "Enter the Medicaid waiver payment amounts excluded from income on Schedule 1, line 8s, unless you choose to include these amounts in earned income, in which case enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    refuses: [scope.other_income]
    note: "Medicaid waiver payments and the Schedule 1 line 8s exclusion are refused (scope.other_income), so this line is zero."

  - id: eic.step5_line3
    printed: "(Step 5, 3)"
    label: "Subtract line 2 from line 1"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: eic.step5_line1, take: eic.step5_line2}

  - id: eic.step5_line4
    printed: "(Step 5, 4)"
    label: "Enter all of your nontaxable combat pay if you elect to include it in earned income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    refuses: [s8812.combat_pay]
    note: "A filer with nontaxable combat pay is refused (s8812.combat_pay), so the election never arises and this line is zero."

  - id: eic.earned_income
    printed: "(Step 5, 5)"
    label: "Add lines 3 and 4. This is your earned income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.step5_line3, eic.step5_line4]}

  - id: eic.self_employed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 5, question 2: Were you self-employed at any time in 2025, or are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or you had church employee income, or are you filing Schedule C as a statutory employee?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [s1.has_sc, {const: true}]}
    note: "Within scope, self-employment means a Schedule C business. Clergy, church employee and statutory employee income are refused elsewhere (scope.schedule1, Schedule C and SE screens)."

  - id: eic.clergy
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 27b: minister, member of a religious order, or Christian Science practitioner filing Schedule SE whose line 2 includes an amount also reported on Form 1040, line 1z"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [{const: true}, {const: false}]}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1]
    note: "Clergy income is outside the tool's scope (refused), so the line 27b box is never checked."

  - id: eic.h_se
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B, Parts 1 and 2: self-employment earned income (line 1e or line 2c) of each person, combined"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.eic_se}
    note: "Open: the per-person catalog field person.eic_se is taken to be that person's Worksheet B Part 1 line 1e (Schedule SE line 3 plus 4b and 5a, minus line 13) or, if not filing SE, Part 2 line 2c (Schedule C line 31). Farm income, the optional method and church income are refused, and Part 3 statutory employee income is screened out, so Part 3 is zero."

  - id: eic.wsb_4b
    printed: "4b"
    label: "Combine lines 1e, 2c, 3, and 4a. This is your total earned income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.h_se, eic.earned_income]}

  - id: eic.wsb_6
    printed: "6 (eic.wsb_6)"
    label: "Enter your total earned income from Part 4, line 4b"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: eic.wsb_4b, take: {const: 0}}
    note: "The line is reached only when line 4b is more than zero (otherwise STOP). It is floored at zero so it is defined in every case; the STOP itself is applied in eic.credit."

  - id: eic.h_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: dependent, field: dependent.one, where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn_child, {const: true}]}}

  - id: eic.h_qc_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of children who meet the conditions to be your qualifying child for the EIC (with or without a valid SSN)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: dependent, field: dependent.one, where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}}

  - id: eic.h_lim0_o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, no children ($19,104)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit0.other}
  - id: eic.h_lim0_j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, no children, married filing jointly ($26,214)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit0.joint}
  - id: eic.h_lim1_o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 1 child ($50,434)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit1.other}
  - id: eic.h_lim1_j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 1 child, married filing jointly ($57,554)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit1.joint}
  - id: eic.h_lim2_o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 2 children ($57,310)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit2.other}
  - id: eic.h_lim2_j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 2 children, married filing jointly ($64,430)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit2.joint}
  - id: eic.h_lim3_o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 3 or more children ($61,555)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit3.other}
  - id: eic.h_lim3_j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 3 or more children, married filing jointly ($68,675)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit3.joint}

  - id: eic.h_lim_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Income limit by number of children with valid SSNs (not married filing jointly)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_lim0_o}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_lim1_o}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc, {const: 2, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_lim2_o}
        - {guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc, {const: 3, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_lim3_o}
  - id: eic.h_lim_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Income limit by number of children with valid SSNs (married filing jointly)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_lim0_j}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_lim1_j}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc, {const: 2, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_lim2_j}
        - {guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc, {const: 3, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_lim3_j}

  - id: eic.h_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Filing a joint return"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}

  - id: eic.h_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Income limit (Step 1 question 1; Step 5 question 3; Worksheet B line 5)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_lim_joint}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_lim_other}

  - id: eic.h_agi_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1, question 1: is the amount on Form 1040, line 11b, less than the limit?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, eic.h_limit]}

  - id: eic.h_7a_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 1040 line 7a (if a loss, enter -0-)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line7a, take: {const: 0}}

  - id: eic.h_inv
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2, question 1: Investment income (lines 2a + 2b + 3b + 7a)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line2a, f1040.line2b, f1040.line3b, eic.h_7a_pos]}

  - id: eic.h_pre
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Steps 1 and 2 passed (income limit, valid SSNs, investment income not more than $11,950)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_agi_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.all_eic_ssn, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, le: [eic.h_inv, {param: eic.investment_limit}]}
    refuses: [scope.other_income, sa.excluded_income, scope.schedule1, scope.other_line16]
    note: "Step 1 questions 3 and 4 (Form 2555, nonresident aliens) are refused. Step 2: investment income over $11,950 leads to the Form 4797 question; Form 4797, Schedule E, rental of personal property, Form 8814 and passive activities are all refused, so the only path left is STOP."

  - id: eic.h_married_ns
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3 question 3 / Step 4 question 1: married taxpayer whose filing status is married filing separately or head of household"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "hoh"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [household.married, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_has_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, question 1: at least one child who meets the conditions to be your qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_qc_any, {const: 0, type: Count}]}

  - id: eic.h_reach6
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3: reached question 6 (not joint; not married-separate/HOH, or passed questions 4 and 5)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_ns, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.same_home, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.legally_separated, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_qc_pass
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3 passed (go to Step 5)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_reach6, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}

  - id: eic.separated_box
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box in the Dependents section that discusses the special rule for separated spouses"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_pre, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_reach6, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_ns, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}
    note: "Step 3 question 6 'No' says to check the box. The Special rule for separated spouses applies only if you are married and not filing a joint return, so the box is limited to married MFS/HOH filers."

  - id: eic.h_age_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4, question 2: persons born after December 31, 1960, and before January 2, 2001"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [person.born_before_2001, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [person.born_after_1960, {const: true}]}
    note: "Open: for a spouse or filer who died in 2025, the text refers to Pub. 596, which is not supplied. The birth-date test is applied as printed."

  - id: eic.h_reach3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4: no qualifying child, passed questions 1 and 2"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_ns, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [eic.h_age_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}

  - id: eic.h_noqc_pass
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4 passed (go to Step 5)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_reach3, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: false}]}
    note: "Step 4 question 6 uses sa.dependent ('Can someone claim you or your spouse as a dependent'), the closest supplied interface."

  - id: eic.h_eligible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Steps 1 through 4 passed"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_pre, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_pass, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_noqc_pass, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.line1
    printed: "1"
    label: "Enter your earned income from Step 5"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: eic.earned_income}

  - id: eic.h_a_t0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, no children, for line 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_a_t1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, one child, for line 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_a_t2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, two children, for line 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_a_t3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, three or more children, for line 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_a_tab
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 1, column for number of children with valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_a_t0}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_a_t1}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc, {const: 2, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_a_t2}
        - {guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc, {const: 3, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_a_t3}
  - id: eic.h_a_in
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 1 is within the EIC Table (at least $1 and less than the limit)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, ge: [eic.line1, {param: eic.table_start}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [eic.line1, eic.h_limit]}

  - id: eic.line2
    printed: "2"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 1 above in the EIC Table to find the credit. Enter the credit here"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_a_in, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_a_tab}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_a_in, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_zero}
    note: "Amounts below $1 or at or above the limit are outside the table and give no credit. The table_lookup status column is assumed to send MFS to the single/HOH/QSS column (★)."

  - id: eic.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11b}

  - id: eic.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Are the amounts on lines 3 and 1 the same?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.line3, eic.line1]}

  - id: eic.h_thr0_o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold, no children ($10,620)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold0.other}
  - id: eic.h_thr0_j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold, no children, married filing jointly ($17,730)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold0.joint}
  - id: eic.h_thr1_o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold, 1 or more children ($23,350)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold1.other}
  - id: eic.h_thr1_j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold, 1 or more children, married filing jointly ($30,470)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold1.joint}
  - id: eic.h_thr_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold (not married filing jointly)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_thr0_o}
        - {guard: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_qc, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_thr1_o}
  - id: eic.h_thr_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold (married filing jointly)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_thr0_j}
        - {guard: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_qc, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_thr1_j}
  - id: eic.h_thr
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_thr_joint}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_thr_other}
  - id: eic.h_below
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5: is the amount on line 3 less than the threshold?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, eic.h_thr]}

  - id: eic.h_g_t0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, no children, for line 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_g_t1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, one child, for line 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_g_t2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, two children, for line 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_g_t3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, three or more children, for line 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_g_tab
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_g_t0}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_g_t1}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc, {const: 2, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_g_t2}
        - {guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc, {const: 3, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_g_t3}
  - id: eic.h_g_in
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 3 is at least the threshold and less than the limit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_below, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, eic.h_limit]}

  - id: eic.line5
    printed: "5"
    label: "If line 3 is not less than the threshold, look up the amount on line 3 in the EIC Table to find the credit. Enter the credit here"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_g_in, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_g_tab}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_g_in, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_zero}
    note: "Shown as zero where the worksheet leaves line 5 blank (below the threshold, or line 4 'Yes'). It is used only when line 4 is 'No' and line 3 is not below the threshold. Line 3 at or above the limit is already a Step 1 STOP."

  - id: eic.h_a_min
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of lines 5 and 2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [eic.line5, eic.line2]}

  - id: eic.line6
    printed: "6 (eic.line6)"
    label: "This is your earned income credit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            any:
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_below, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.line2
        - guard:
            op: test
            not:
              op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_below, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_a_min

  - id: eic.h_b_t0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 7: EIC Table, no children, for line 6"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.wsb_6, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_b_t1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 7: EIC Table, one child, for line 6"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.wsb_6, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_b_t2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 7: EIC Table, two children, for line 6"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.wsb_6, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_b_t3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 7: EIC Table, three or more children, for line 6"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.wsb_6, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_b_tab
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 7: EIC Table credit for line 6"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_b_t0}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_b_t1}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc, {const: 2, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_b_t2}
        - {guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc, {const: 3, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_b_t3}
  - id: eic.h_b_in
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 6 is within the EIC Table"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, ge: [eic.wsb_6, {param: eic.table_start}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [eic.wsb_6, eic.h_limit]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 7: Look up the amount on line 6 in the EIC Table to find the credit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_b_in, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_b_tab}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_b_in, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_zero}
  - id: eic.h_wsb9
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 9: Are the amounts on lines 8 and 6 the same?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.line3, eic.wsb_6]}
    note: "Worksheet B line 8 (Form 1040 line 11b) equals eic.line3, and its line 10 equals eic.line5, so both are reused."
  - id: eic.h_wsb_min
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B: smaller of lines 10 and 7"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [eic.line5, eic.h_wsb7]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb11
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 11: This is your earned income credit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            any:
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb9, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_below, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb7
        - guard:
            op: test
            not:
              op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb9, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_below, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_min

  - id: eic.h_path_a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet A applies and Step 5 question 3 passed (earned income less than the limit)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eligible, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [eic.earned_income, eic.h_limit]}
  - id: eic.h_path_b
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B applies, line 4b is more than zero, and line 5 'Yes'"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eligible, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [eic.wsb_4b, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [eic.wsb_4b, eic.h_limit]}

  - id: eic.credit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income credit (Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 27a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_path_a, {const: true}]}, then: eic.line6}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_path_b, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_wsb11}
        - guard:
            op: test
            not:
              op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_path_a, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_path_b, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_zero
    refuses: [household.credit_disallowed]
    note: "Every STOP in Steps 1-5 and Worksheets A/B gives zero. A filer whose EIC was earlier reduced or disallowed (Form 8862) is refused. The 'IRS figures the credit' option is not modeled."

  - id: eic.check_27c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box on line 27c (instructed to check it in the line 27a instructions)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_agi_ok, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [household.all_eic_ssn, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_pre, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_reach6, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_pre, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_reach3, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: false}]}
                - op: test
                  all:
                    - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
                    - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_path_a, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.line2, {const: 0}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eligible, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, le: [eic.wsb_4b, {const: 0}]}
                - {op: test, ge: [eic.wsb_4b, eic.h_limit]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_path_b, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb7, {const: 0}]}
    note: "Covers each STOP that tells you to check line 27c: Step 1 question 2; Step 3 question 6; Step 4 questions 3 and 5; Worksheet A line 2 zero; Worksheet B line 4b zero or less, line 5 'No', and line 7 zero. The nonresident-alien case is refused. The filer's choice not to claim the credit has no supplied input, so it is not modeled."
